  "raw_model_response": "To solve this task, I need to determine the total amount paid by the manager for the dinner at Whimsical Bites, including Jordan's share. Here's my approach:\n\n1. **Identify the Venmo transactions related to the dinner**: I'll use the Venmo app to find transactions related to the dinner. Since the manager paid for the food and everyone Venmoed them, I'll look for transactions with the manager's email address as the recipient.\n\n2. **Filter out Jordan's transaction**: Jordan's share was $34, so I'll need to exclude this from the total.\n\n3. **Calculate the total amount paid by the manager**: I'll sum up all the transactions related to the dinner, excluding Jordan's, and add Jordan's share to get the total amount paid by the manager.\n\nLet me start by exploring the Venmo APIs to find the relevant transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_descriptions(app_name='venmo'))\n```",

  "prompt_tokens": 2523,
  "raw_model_response": "To solve this task, I need to determine the total amount my manager paid for the dinner, including my share. Here's my approach:\n\n1. **Identify the Venmo transactions**: I need to find the Venmo transactions related to the dinner at Whimsical Bites. Since the manager paid for the food and everyone Venmoed them, I should look for transactions with the manager's email address as the recipient.\n\n2. **Filter out my own transaction**: I need to exclude my own transaction, which is $34, from the total amount.\n\n3. **Calculate the total amount paid by the manager**: Sum up all the transactions related to the dinner, including my share, and then subtract my share to get the amount paid by the manager for the others.\n\nLet me start by exploring the Venmo API to see what information I can get.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_descriptions(app_name='venmo'))\n```",

## Interpretation

- **VERIFIED:** task outcomes, actions, observations, and first divergences are from immutable raw rows.
- **INFERENCE:** N1-N2 differences are reported directionally; they are not presumed to be beneficial or memory-specific.
- **UNVERIFIED:** mechanisms downstream of the first recorded behavioral divergence.
